NO LEAKS FOUND: all core stores are bounded capped arrays with in-place compaction (rocks 24 + belt 5, bullets 64, enemy bolts 24, mines 10, drones 6, drops 1; transients - mine pendingFires, drone decayed, Splicer _pending, vortex pending, the ST-074 event double buffer - all drained or reset per tick); render layer fully pooled (mesh pools sized from the same CONFIG keys, one 512-slot THREE.Points ring for all particles, 12 pre-created score-popup divs, kill feed <= 5 rows). Browser 600 s organic soak on the REAL bloom path (?nocache=185a, real Enter, 15 waves / 3 bosses / belt / drones / vortices, 648 kills, 7157 fires, 121 samples every 5 sim-s): GPU resources step up ONLY on the first render of each content class then PLATEAU (geoms 14 -> 30 by t=390 s, tex 15 -> 22, zero steps in the final 210 s - no leak signature); JS heap a bounded GC sawtooth (12.9-25.3 MB) with a STABLE floor (per-100 s minima 12854/14940/15967/15119/14748/15777 KB, no drift; peaks content- + SFX-churn-driven); DOM constant (29 body children = MENU baseline, kill-feed rows <= 5, popups fixed 12); zero cap/transient/DOM violations across all samples; console 0/0; ship/rocks/boss finite at end. New 6 unit tests (tests/memoryBounds.test.js) pin the invariants ST-077/ST-138/I129/I144 left open: mine cap hammer, pendingFires drain discipline, Splicer _pending per-tick bound + drain-to-empty, event double buffer fully clear after two drains, rocks+belt combined ceiling = the asteroidRenderer pool size (24+5), powerup render-pool contract (powerupMesh pool 4 >= maxActiveDrops). 581/581 (+6); build 0; no production code changed (the audit confirms the architecture). Method note: renderer.info.memory in this three.js build is a persistent live-resource count (WebGLInfo.reset() zeros only render stats - verified in vendor source), sampled without reset. Labeled as a hierarchy-consistency + grounding call from counter data + the ladder, NOT a human feel measurement (the I202 playtest is the confirmation pass, with __vr.juiceTally() as its baseline). 10 new unit tests (tests/juiceTally.test.js: schema, empty, per-call count, accumulation, no-row, unknown-key, invalid args, reset, total, snapshot deep-copy). - I180: Tests: audio headless test suite (WebAudio stub) (ST-180) - src/render/audio.js (the 26-cue synth layer) previously had zero automated coverage: every behavior (rate limiting, pan, mute, master chain) was hand-smoked only. New tests/helpers/webaudio.js (test-only fixture - the in-process runner only loads tests/*.test.js and the build only bundles src/+assets/, so it is never shipped or executed) installs a stub AudioContext on global.window implementing the full WebAudio surface audio.js uses (gain/compressor/analyser/oscillator/bufferSource/panner/biquad/buffer) and records every setTargetAtTime into __lastTarget so volume/mute/retargeting are assertable deterministically (analyser returns a flat 0.25 frame -> peak = rms = 0.25 exact). New tests/audio.test.js: 18 tests - state/meter/sfx pre-init no-ops; weights() maps all 26 cues to finite positive gains (pins the I173 tiers droneDecay 0.14 / wardenSpawn 0.22 / wardenDeath 0.34 / slowmo 0.18 + ST-087 impact trims bomb 0.55 / hit 0.45); init() builds gain->comp->makeup->limiter->analyser/dest with the exact compressor/limiter settings + the 0.5s noise buffer; init() idempotency + suspended-resume; sfx counting; stereo pan from world x (+-ARENA_R clamp * 0.8, non-finite x -> center); per-cue rate limit (shoot min 0.04); setVolume clamp [0,1]; setMuted master-gain routing; setSfx gating; timeWarp cents mapping (0.5 -> -350, clamped); musicIntensity clamp [0,1]; musicChord (Am, index 0); meter peak/rms math; cue node creation (tone+noise vs 4-note stinger, no noise source). Deliberate boundary: music(true)/ambience(true) are NEVER called (recurring setTimeouts + 1.2-1.3s disconnect tails would hang the in-process runner); the bus seams are exercised in the no-bus state main.js starts them in. Parse node --check clean (both files); 565/565 (+18); build 0. - I172: E8E audio: bullet-time audio (ST-172) - the slow-mo power-up (ST-115) now carries the Max-Payne-lineage aural treatment, grounded in YouTube research (Arin Eakins "Creating Bullet Time Effects in FMOD" snapshot-instrument technique; Aftertouch Audio "Sniper Ghost Warrior Contracts Slow Motion Bullet" SFX demo; Tom Player Music "Bullet time" Matrix-style track - metadata/chapters verified, transcripts rate-limited, see design/audio-music.md Coverage note): while the world is dilated the drone bed drops in pitch + darkens, then glides back. New src/core/timeWarp.js (pure, DOM-free): timeWarpX(scale) = clamp(1-scale, 0, 1) maps the game seam world.enemyScale() (1 normally, 0.4 while slow-mo is active) to a dilation amount x (slow-mo => x 0.6); timeWarpCents(x, {centsMax:700}) = -x*centsMax => slow-mo lands at exactly -420 cents (a bit over a major third down; the proportional -1586 rejected as sub-bass/inadvisable - real bullet-time cues drop by semitones/thirds); x=0 returns an explicit 0 (assert/strict uses Object.is, -0 trap). Tunables CONFIG.music.warp {centsMax: 700, filterKeep: 0.3} (src/core/config.js). Render (src/render/audio.js): audio.timeWarp(x, {centsMax, filterKeep}) retargets pad-osc + sub-drone o.detune (separate AudioParam: composes with the I169 18s chord glides), pluck inherits cents at creation, lowpass retarget = (1200 + 1400*intensity) * (1 - x*(1-keep)) composing with the I170 intensity opening; all setTargetAtTime(tau 0.25); no new nodes/timers (menu-safe no-op while the bed is stopped). Wiring (src/main.js syncVisuals): PLAYING-gated wx = timeWarpX(world.enemyScale()), change-cached |dx| > 0.02 (I167/I170 discipline), _timeWarp resets to 0 off-PLAYING so a (re)start re-applies from rest; +2 __vr probes (timeWarp, warpCents). Seam correction: the handoff referenced world.game.enemyScale(), but world.game is the GameState machine - the method lives on World (src/core/game.js), so the wiring calls world.enemyScale(). New tests/timeWarp.test.js (7 tests: curve, clamps, explicit 0, -0 guard, custom centsMax, degenerate inputs). - I169: E8E audio: procedural ambient drone loop (ST-169) - I158's static 4-note A-minor saw pad (held constant all run) + a C-major pentatonic pluck that tonally clashed with the A-minor pad, re-built (src/render/audio.js) into an EVOLVING ambient drone loop grounded in YouTube research (Marshall McGee ambient sound, The Ground Noise dark-ambient-drone recipe, Simon Hutchinson procedural-ambience rule - metadata/chapters verified, transcripts rate-limited, see design/audio-music.md Coverage note). The bed now cycles a 4-chord Am-Fmaj7-Cmaj7-G loop (18s each = 72s, no seam - it returns to Am) via setTargetAtTime frequency GLIDES (tau 2.5s): 4 pad osc + 4 gain, a sine sub-drone at chord-root/2 with 0.1Hz breathing tremolo, a looped-noise 'air' layer (shared 0.5s noiseBuf -> bandpass 400Hz Q0.5 gain 0.02, 0.03Hz LFO wandering +/-150Hz), pluck re-tuned to A-minor pentatonic, existing 0.05Hz lowpass-sweep LFO kept. No node churn on a chord change (only at start/stop; ~17 nodes + 2 repeating timers, all cleared on stop = no pause/resume leak). New musicIntensity(x) [0..1] is the ST-170 escalation seam (filter 1200->2600Hz, bus gain 0.13->0.18, smooth setTargetAtTime) - UNWIRED to gameplay; ST-170 owns the world-state->x mapping. - I162: E8D test batch - stats storage, hotkey contract, codec hardening (ST-162) - 21 new node --test tests (tests/e8dBatch.test.js) pinning existing production behavior for the E8D surface (cgs-standards-test-standards; zero production code changed). CODEC (share.js, ST-142): encode clamps out-of-range/NaN/undefined to the safe floor (seed 0 / score 0 / wave 1 / kills 0); decode rejects a tampered body (checksum mismatch), a tampered checksum, 5- and 7-field strings, and null/number/object/empty/upper-case input; custom-magic round-trips and cross-magic decode fails in both directions; max representable values (seed 0xffffffff, score/wave/kills 0x7fffffff) round-trip; the FNV-1a checksum is always exactly 2 zero-padded base36 chars (padding case observed across 200 seeds); 25 distinct seeds -> 25 distinct valid codes, each decoding back. STATS STORAGE (storage.js): recordScore ranks ties after strictly-greater entries only (second 50 on a [100,50] board -> rank 2, never newBest); full 5-slot board + lower score -> rank 0 with the board unchanged; non-finite wave/kills sanitize to 1/0 and score <= 0 is ignored; newBest true only at rank 1; setSettings discards unknown keys from the partial and resets an invalid fx ('medium') to the default tier (dropping the earlier 'low'); the written payload is exactly {version, ...clean} with the known keys only; a corrupt daily payload degrades (invalid JSON -> null, empty v1 -> {key:'', value:0}); recordDaily raises only on higher same-day scores and rolls over on a new day; getBest/setBest fall back to 0 for non-finite/non-numeric/negative stored values. HOTKEY CONTRACT (F, ST-157): toggleFxQuality has strict parity over 9 flips plus the defensive non-'low' -> 'low' fallback; setSettings({fx:'low'}) persists, survives a simulated reload (fresh Storage over the same store), and leaves volume/sfx/reducedMotion untouched. 491/491 (was 470), build 0. No rendering change - no browser smoke. ST-162 closed. Next I163 ST-163 browser verify E8D (all screens).

- I152: session stats - accuracy metric + sessionStats API (ST-152). ScoreKeeper now tracks shotsFired/shotsHit (onShotFired(n) floors + clamps bad input, onShotHit(), accuracy() = min(1, hits/fired) with 0 before the first shot, reset() clears); World.onPlayerBulletHit(bulletId) wraps weapons.markHit + onShotHit so all 5 player-bullet hit sites (rocks, mines, boss, warden, drones) count in one place (enemy bolts not counted); World counts onShotFired(shots.length) at the muzzle (spread fires count n at once); new World.sessionStats() snapshot { score, kills, wave, maxCombo, shotsFired, shotsHit, accuracy, time }; game-over screen gains a third stats line "ACCURACY % SHOTS h/f" (cyan, panel style). +4 unit tests (accuracy zero before any shot; rises and caps at 1; onShotFired floors/clamps/ignores bad input; reset clears). 466/466 (was 462), build 0. - I151: first-run tutorial overlay (ST-151) - opens E8D (UI/UX). A one-time, richer, dismissible "how to play" card (new src/render/tutorialOverlay.js, mirrors the screens.js panel/keycap style, reduced-motion aware via vr-reduced) shown on the player's first entry to PLAYING (distinct from the passive 6s tutorialHint). While visible it gates the sim - tick() early-returns so world.time is frozen (advance(5) leaves time at 0) while rendering keeps going - so the player reads without dying; dismiss via the LAUNCH button or Enter/Space/Esc (keydown guard owns the keys, togglePause no-ops while active). Dismissal persists in a dedicated storage key (Storage.getTutorialSeen/setTutorialSeen, versioned payload, mirrors the daily pattern, no config change - constructor default key 'voidrunner.tutorial'); on subsequent runs the passive hint takes over. startRun branches on !getTutorialSeen(); the overlay is exposed on window.__vr (tutorial, tutorialActive(), dismissTutorial). Browser smoke (Playwright ?nocache=151): fresh first-run shows the centered card (screenshot .evidence/I151/I151_tutorial_shown.png), Enter AND button-click both dismiss + persist {"version":1,"seen":true}, reload does NOT re-show (passive hint shows, sim runs), sim resumes after dismiss, console 0/0, deep NaN scan clean (only the intentional lastKillTime=-Infinity sentinel). +3 unit tests for the storage seen-flag (idempotent + corrupt/unknown fallback + injected key). 462/462 (was 459), build 0. ST-151 closed. Next I152 ST-152 session stats.

- I150: E8C GDD addendum + changelog review (ST-150) - closes epic E8C on docs (no code/test). design/gdd.md now documents the four E8C systems that had no GDD section (config.js carried authority until now): The Splicer (ST-143) - boss variant 2, orbits the arena center at 11u, 1.2s entrance (no bolts until entered>=1), continuous two-arm spiral (spiralEvery 0.6s, 2 arms, armSpin 1.2 rad/s, bolts 7u/s) + aimed bolt (2.6s), NO rock rings / NO radial burst, base 30 HP with wave-15 +8 per 5-wave up to cap 72 (steps=floor((wave-15)/5)), radius 2.6, +600 (Warden 400 < Splicer 600 < Monolith 1000), splits 2 M, one-way phase 2 at 50% (orbit 0.7 / armSpin 1.8 / spiralEvery 0.39 / aimEvery 1.82 / spin 1.2), seeded orbit direction; Boss Variant Alternation (ST-143) - even bossSpawnCount = Monolith, odd = Splicer, the count increments only on an actual spawn so a skipped 5th wave preserves the alternation, rim spawn re-rolled 8x if <10u from the ship, bossSpawn event carries kind; Daily Seeded Run (ST-140/141) - dailySeed = fnv1a('daily:YYYY-MM-DD') (32-bit FNV-1a, no crypto), 'daily' salt namespaces, DAILY RUN menu button + badge + daily best, restart re-seeds the stored seed (2026-11-10 -> 317547475); Shareable Result String (ST-142) - encodeResult -> vr1.<seed36>.<score36>.<wave36>.<kills36>.<ck>, ck = fnv1a(body)%1296 as 2-char base36, decodeResult validates 6 parts / magic / base36+dot charset / checksum / ranges, seed included so results are reproducible (vr1.592577.l0x.5.3c.6g = the 2026-11-10 daily result). Also fixed the WARDEN GDD bullet (the 5th-wave heavyweight is now a BOSS - Monolith OR Splicer - not just Monolith) and added a kill-base-score ladder to Scoring (L25/M50/S100, golden 5x, mine 150, seeker 200, Warden 400, Splicer 600, Monolith 1000). Every number cross-checked against src/core/config.js + splicer.js/daily.js/share.js/game.js (cgs-consistency-check) - no drift. Changelog E8C entries I133-I149 reviewed: ordered, consistent, no gaps. 459/459 fresh, build 0. ST-150 closed; epic E8C closed. Next I151 ST-151 first-run tutorial overlay (E8D UI/UX begins).

- I145: test batch E8C - 28 cross-system invariant tests (ST-145) - new tests/e8cBatch.test.js locks the E8-2 cross-system contracts: boss alternation (skips do not advance the variant count; variant order preserved; bossSpawn event kind), heavyweight exclusivity (a live boss of either kind blocks the Warden; one Warden at a time), rim spawn inside the arena (seeds 1-10, ship on the ring, tries loop terminates), HP/score family (per-variant HP at every 5-wave spawn; Splicer <= Monolith at shared waves; hpFirstWave boundary = base HP; score ladder warden < splicer < monolith), enemyBullets pool pressure (60 s sustained Splicer phase 2 under the 24 cap; fire() null exactly at the cap and a cull frees a slot; a bolt passes through a belt rock - no bolt-vs-rock collision; belt + spiral + Warden coexist), determinism (bit-identical seeded worlds across the Monolith -> Splicer script, skip + respawn - the skip consumes no RNG, and a daily-seeded run through both bosses), edge cases (aim gating before aimEvery, spiral coverage > 5 rad, the family ctor hp contract, skipped waves touch neither the Warden slot nor the belt layer). One test fixed while authoring (the belt pass-through test had an inverted distance assertion - the bolt ends 2.48 u past the rock, so the old check could never hold; replaced with full-distance/velocity/far-side + rock-untouched checks). 456/456 (was 428, +28), build 0, tests-only (no smoke). Next I146 ST-146 browser verify E8C.

- I144: balance pass E8-2 (boss family) (cgs-balance-check) (ST-144) - audited Monolith/Warden/Splicer together (HP curves vs weapon ramp, score ladder, enemyBullets pool pressure, spiral dodgeability). Found + fixed the Splicer phase-1 spiral being unthreadable: the gap between consecutive bolts along an arm = boltSpeed x spiralEvery = 1.82u < the ship's 2.24u effective obstacle (diameter + 2 bolt radii), and the 7.7 bolts/s rate exceeded the 24-bolt pool cap (fire() silently dropped ~22% of the spiral). Fix (2 values, no new tunable): splicer.spiralEvery 0.26 -> 0.6 (gap 4.2u = 1.88x obstacle, threadable; 3.3 bolts/s) + splicer.phase2.spiralEveryMult 0.6 -> 0.65 (enrage gap 2.73u = 1.22x obstacle, tight but fair; 5.1 bolts/s). Monolith/Warden unchanged (already tuned I133/I134/I136). +2 invariant tests (gap-vs-obstacle both phases; worst-case pool occupancy p1 14.9 / p2 22.7 < cap 24). design/balance-e8-2.md audit (family table, pool pressure, exploit risk, pacing). 428/428 (was 426), build 0, browser smoke ?nocache=144 (p1 live bolts 9-12, p2 15-20 - under cap, no drops; hit() 30 -> 14 triggers phase 2; 0 NaN, console 0/0; .evidence/I144/I144_spiral_gap.png). Next I145 ST-145 test batch E8C (25+ new).

- I143: boss variant 2 'the Splicer' (core+render) (ST-143) - new src/core/splicer.js (pure, DOM-free): a second boss kind alternating with the Monolith every boss spawn (game.js spawnBossForWave alternates on bossSpawnCount%2). The Splicer glides from the rim to a hold radius (11 u) and orbits slowly around the arena center; instead of rock rings or the radial burst (Monolith only) it emits a continuous two-arm spiral of undestroyable bolts (drainBolts() -> World fires them into the enemyBullets pool at boltSpeed) plus a slow aimed bolt; one-way enrage phase 2 below 50% hp (faster orbit/arm spin/spin, tighter spiral, shorter aim cadence); 1 damage per hit; score 600; death splits 2 M rocks. splicerHpForWave mirrors the Monolith HP curve over base 30 / cap 72. boss.js: Monolith ctor gains kind/score/splitCount/splitSize (one shared boss contract). Config: config.splicer {maxHp 30, hpFirstWave 15, hpStep 8, hpCap 72, radius 2.6, score 600, splitCount 2, splitSize 'M', holdRadius 11, orbitSpeed 0.5, spin 0.4, enterTime 1.2, arms 2, armSpin 1.2, spiralEvery 0.26, boltSpeed 7.0, aimEvery 2.6, phase2 {hpFrac 0.5, orbitSpeedMult 1.4, armSpinMult 1.5, spiralEveryMult 0.6, aimEveryMult 0.7, spin 1.2}}. Render: bossMesh teal emissive 0x2dd4bf + faster pulse + sizeScale 0.765; bossHealth 'SPLICER' label + teal bar; waveBanner name param ('THE SPLICER'); main.js per-kind banner + 'SPLICER INBOUND' toast. 426/426 (was 415, +11 tests/splicer.test.js), build 0, browser smoke ?nocache=143b (forced Splicer: kind 'splicer', maxHp 30, 8 spiral bolts @ 7.0 u/s, teal emissive 0x2dd4bf, label 'SPLICER', 0 NaN, console 0/0; .evidence/I143/I143_splicer.png). Next I144 ST-144 balance pass E8-2 (cgs-balance-check).

- I142: shareable result codec (core) (ST-142) - new src/core/share.js (pure, DOM-free): encodeResult({score,wave,seed,kills}, magic?) -> compact URL-safe string 'vr1.<b36 seed>.<b36 score>.<b36 wave>.<b36 kills>.<2-char checksum>' (reuses daily.js FNV-1a); decodeResult(str, magic?) -> {ok,seed,score,wave,kills}|{ok:false} with full validation (string, 6 dot-parts, magic, base36+dot charset, checksum, ranges seed<=0xffffffff/score>=0/wave>=1/kills>=0). config.share {magic 'vr1'} namespaces the codec. A player shares a result so others replay the identical board (seed) and see the score; the copy-to-clipboard UI is a later E8D iteration. 415/415 (was 405, +10 tests/share.test.js), build 0, core-only (no browser smoke). - I141: daily UI (browser) (ST-141) - wired the ST-140 daily seed into the UI: a DAILY RUN button in the menu (keyboard + touch, aria-labelled) starts a run seeded from today (dailyForDate) so every player today plays the identical board (comparable daily leaderboard). Core: World.reseed(seed) (game.js) resets the shared RNG so a run starts from a specific seed (chainable); storage.js gains getDailyBest()/recordDaily(key, score) (per-calendar-day daily best - raises today's, rolls over on a new day, ignores zero, corrupt-safe). main.js: daily state + startRun/startDaily/restartRun (Enter=normal, DAILY=daily, restart re-seeds the SAME stored seed to reproduce today's run even across midnight; records the daily best on game over). screens.js: DAILY button + menu daily line ('DAILY <key>  -  BEST <n>' / 'NO BEST YET') + pill DAILY badge on game-over (with NEW DAILY BEST). 405/405 (was 399, +6: reseed determinism x2, storage daily best x4), build 0, browser smoke ?nocache=141 (menu DAILY button + 'DAILY 2026-11-10  -  NO BEST YET'; click -> PLAYING + rng.seed == FNV-1a('daily:2026-11-10')=317547475; forced game over -> 'DAILY RUN  -  2026-11-10' badge; 0 NaN, console 0/0; .evidence/I141/{menu,gameover}.png). Next I142 ST-142 shareable result string (score+seed+wave, encode/decode, unit codec round-trip).

The seed is just a number fed to the existing RNG/World (no core logic touched); the menu button + DAILY badge are I141. 399/399 (was 389, +10 tests/daily.test.js: config shape, fnv1a unsigned+deterministic+input-sensitive, key zero-pad, seed range/determinism, 7 distinct days -> 7 distinct seeds, salt namespacing, dailyForDate 1-based-month parity, identical RNG stream, and bit-identical 400-step World run from the same daily seed), build 0. - I131: enemy spawn-mix rebalance - data-driven table + late-game L floor (ST-131) - the spawn size mix was hardcoded in pickSize() (S base 0.3, +0.02/wave from w10, 0.45 cap, L:M 3:4 within non-S) while the GDD fixes split rules but not spawn ratios. Now config.spawnMix.rows (4 rows: w1-2 50/50/0 teach, w3-9 0.30/0.40/0.30 = pre-I131 exactly, w10-14 0.28/0.32/0.40, w15+ 0.30/0.30/0.40) + lFloor 0.28 invariant drives mixRow()/pickSize() (draw widths unchanged, so w1-9 seeded behavior is bit-identical); waveEndless sBias keys deleted. Playtest data: S kills are split-supplied (w14 72% of kills vs 49% of spawns) so the old S ramp was a weak lever, while the L share had eroded 50% -> 24% by w17+ (core "break the big rock" loop fading). A/B (10 full-run seeds + forced w10-16): all full runs bit-identical (bot dies w5-w8, known boss-ring limit), forced waves 21/21 hits identical across arms (no survival shift), realized late mix L 28.4 -> 33.5 pp with S flat ~38.9. Harness: per-size spawn/kill tallies + oldmix arm; fixed a pre-existing bot null-thrust crash (only eb/mine threats in range). 345/345 (was 337, +8 in tests/spawnMix.test.js; 2 endless tests updated to the table contract), build 0. next I132 ST-132 E8B GDD addendum (drone section + I130 drop table + I131 spawnMix table).
- I130: power-up drop rebalance - weighted 7-type table + drop lifetime (ST-130) - the I129 playtest data showed the realized drop rate was 1.0% of kills vs the nominal 25% roll: with maxActiveDrops=1 and no lifetime, one uncollected drop clogged the pool forever. Fixes (config + powerups.js): (1) powerup.dropLife = 20 s - abandoned drops expire (full unscaled dt, player-side) and free the pool, with a scale-based shrink-out over the last fadeTime = 2.5 s in powerupMesh.js (shared per-type materials stay pooled); (2) powerup.weights {rapid 20, spread 20, shield 15, magnet 15, bomb 10, overdrive 10, slowmo 10} replace the uniform 1/7 roll via pickType() (one rng.next() draw, zero-weight types never roll, rescale over sum). Playtest A/B (same seeds, emergent): realized rate 0.012-0.019 -> 0.024-0.035, seed 33 pickups 2 -> 7 (157 -> 621 kills), 5-6 of 7 types sampled per run vs 2-4 before; residual gap vs the 25% roll is lifetime/cap physics by design. 337/337 (was 330, +7 in tests/dropTable.test.js: table shape, determinism, 20k-draw distribution, zero-weight rescale, exact expiry + pool freed, pickup-before-expiry, no RNG perturbation at cap), build 0, browser smoke (fade + expiry + pool freed + weighted types) console 0/0 + 0 NaN, screenshot .evidence/I130_drop_fade.png. next I131 ST-131 enemy spawn-mix rebalance (mix table).
- I129: drone.maxAge - seeker battery self-destruct, post-gate playtest balance follow-up (ST-129) - acted on the post-gate playtest battery (design/balance-e8b.md "Post-gate playtest (I129)" - deterministic headless bot, 8 forced-wave scenarios + 3 full runs): homing drones accumulated unboundedly (1/wave from 8, cap 6, no despawn) and spiked onto boss waves (forced w15 = boss + 6 cumulative drones was the only forced wave the bot died on). Fix: drone.maxAge = 30 (game-time s, slow-mo scaled) - a drone self-destructs when its battery runs down (droneDecay event -> cyan fizzle + seeker-zap cue, no shake, honors FX tier off), bounding the live pool to the last ~1-2 waves. Before/after arms (scripts/playtest.mjs, pre-fix arm = maxAge=Infinity, cumulative drones pre-aged 18 s/wave): forced w15 3 hits / 17.9 s / 6 drone kills -> 3 hits / 15.8 s / 2 drone kills + 4 decays on wave entry (boss wave now loads like boss + 2 young drones = the pre-fix survivable w10 experience); forced w10 2 -> 1 hit on w10, run survives to w15 vs w13. Fresh-drone scenarios unchanged (30 s battery > 14-32 s wave window, as expected). New deterministic harness scripts/playtest.mjs (droneDecays tally + pre-aged cumulative drones + pre-fix arm). 330/330 (was 324, +6 in tests/droneMaxAge.test.js: decay exactly at maxAge, slot freed, no double-decay, slow-mo dt semantics, world event at position, deterministic decay step), build 0, browser smoke (droneDecay FX) console 0/0 + 0 NaN, screenshot .evidence/I129_drone_decay.png. next I130 ST-130 power-up drop rebalance (7 types).

- I124: balance pass E8-1 - audit + bomb/genBonus parity fix (ST-124) - evidence before tuning: audited the full economy (config.js + game.js scoring path); the E8B systems (weapon levels, shielded rocks, split gen bonus, drones) are well-balanced with NO exploitable inflation (DPS 5.5->11->~19 bullets/s tracks the rock-count/speed ramp; per-kill value is fixed so higher DPS = intended progression; near-miss is per-approach; golden + genBonus mutually exclusive; combo cap 2.4x + overdrive x2 bound the ceiling). The one genuine gap: the bullet path applied the split per-gen bonus but the BOMB path did not, so a gen-2 S cleared by bomb under-scored (100 vs 150); fixed game.js bombBlast to apply the same (1 + genBonus) multiplier (still flat 400 for shielded). +4 unit tests (tests/bombGenBonus.test.js: bomb gen-1 M=62, gen-2 S=150, bomb/bullet parity, shielded bomb=flat 400); new design/balance-e8b.md audit. 283/283, build 0, seq 1..124; doc+node (no render). next I125 determinism re-verify (node).

## [0.2.0] - 2026-11-13 (M1: vertical slice SHIP)
### Added
- I01: project scaffold (Vite + three.js), renderer boot, polar grid arena, resize handling.
- I02: seeded RNG (mulberry32) + math utils (damp, v2 helpers, angle helpers) with unit tests.
- I03: central CONFIG table (arena/ship/weapon/asteroid/wave/combo/powerup/fx/storage) with schema tests.
- I04: procedural two-shell starfield (soft additive glow sprites, seeded) + scene fog + hemisphere lighting.
- Plan extended to 100 iterations (E7: extended content, UI/UX, hardening, second QA/release cycle).
- I05: ship core (inertial movement, arena clamp, damped aim, invuln/knockback) + 7 unit tests.
- I06: procedural ship mesh, mouse raycast aim, follow camera with look-ahead, keyboard/mouse input state.
- I07: weapons core (bullet pool/cap, cadence, spread, culling) + 8 unit tests.
- I08: pooled bullet rendering + Space/click firing wired to aim.
- I09: asteroid field core (rim spawn, bounce, splits, caps, seeded) + 7 unit tests.
- I10: asteroid rendering (noise-baked low-poly variants, pooled, tumble).
- I11: collision helpers (circles, bullet/ship vs rocks) + 6 unit tests.
- I12: World orchestrator (full collision loop, events), fixed-timestep sim, deterministic advance() hook.
- I13: score + combo chain (window/cap/step, damage+timeout reset) wired into World; 7 new unit tests.
- I14: wave director (escalating ramp, trickle spawn, intermission, clear detection) + 7 unit tests.
- I15: lives manager + state machine (MENU/PLAYING/PAUSED/GAME_OVER) + 8 unit tests.
- I16: DOM HUD (score/wave/lives/combo meter, change-cached text).
- I17: menu/pause/game-over screens, Enter/P-Esc/M session keys, no auto-start on load.
### Fixed
- I17: World.reset() crash (assignment to gameOver getter) caught by new tests.
- I18: persistent best score + settings (injected store, guarded reads, NEW BEST flag, survives reload).
- I19: power-up field core (drops, drift, magnet pull, timed effects, instant bomb queue) wired into World.
- I20: power-up render meshes (pooled, per-type shape/color, hover + pulse).
- I21: RAPID + SPREAD fire path — power-ups modify cadence (x2 for 8s) and 3-way spread burst.
- I22: SHIELD absorption — cyan ring around ship, absorbs 1 hit with pop flash (GDD-compliant).
- I23: BOMB shatter-all (per-rock kills feed combo) + MAGNET bullet homing (slight, 6s).
- I24: pooled particle bursts on kill/hit/shield-pop/bomb (FX-tier aware, reduced-motion aware).
- I25: screen shake, hit flash and invulnerability blink (FX-tier + reduced-motion aware).
- I26: settings panel (volume, sfx, fx tier, reduced motion) with live persistence.
- I27: synthesized WebAudio SFX for all gameplay events (gesture-init, volume/sfx/mute aware).
- I28: wave banner + intermission countdown.
- I29: audio polish - compressor, stereo pan, pitch jitter, wave-clear and game-over stingers.
- I30: floating score popups (+points) at kill position; big gold for combo-multiplier kills.
- I31: game-over stats (kills, max combo, time survived, wave reached).
- I32: tutorial hint on session start (controls line, fades after 6s, reduced-motion aware).
- I33: touch controls (drag thrust + fire, tap fire with guaranteed bullet, two-finger pause).
- I34: spawn safety (rim rocks re-roll to stay 8+ units from the ship) + balance pass 1 (combo window 3.5 -> 4s, power-up drop 22% -> 25%).
- I35: bloom post-fx on High quality (UnrealBloomPass via composer); Low disables bloom and keeps the direct render path.
- I36: ?fps=1/?fps=2 performance meter + perf pass (idle-frame particle upload skip, per-frame allocation reuse, accurate multi-pass GPU stats).
- I37: fix stray Enter mid-game re-showing the tutorial hint and clearing score popups; Enter now only acts on MENU/GAME_OVER transitions.
- I38: code review pass (full source, cgs-code-review contract) - verdict APPROVE, security clean; magnet pull acceleration moved to config as powerup.magnetAccel (behavior identical); tab-blur auto-pause + WebGL context-loss recovery deferred to I43.
- I39: balance pass 2 - wave speed cap 1.9 -> 2.1 (ramp now runs to wave 18; counterweights the I34 sustain buffs); GDD + balance notes updated, wave cap test made config-derived.
- I40: QA plan (production/qa-plan.md) - risk ranking, test matrix, regression suites A/B/C, slice-gate entry/exit criteria; Suite A + B executed green (critical path 8/8, combo math verified in-situ).
- I41: playtest report (scripted self-play, 2 skill profiles) - wave-1 stall gap (F1) + hit-clustering watch (F2) flagged; no bugs found.
- I42: bugfix B - wave stall advance (wave.stallTime 45s): an uncleared wave now advances anyway with leftover asteroids carried over; closes playtest F1 (camped wave-1 stall).
- I43: edge cases - visibilitychange auto-pause, WebGL context-loss auto-pause + restore recovery, #flash null guard, 5-min NaN soak (closes I38 C3/C4/C5).
- I44: UI/UX pass - HUD power-up chips (shape+color+label, drain bars), combo window drain bar, fire-line reticle (playtest rec #3), menu keycaps + pause session snapshot, contrast bumps (ST-044).
- I45: vertical slice gate - QA Suite C executed green (storage round-trip, resize, touch synth, I43 robustness re-verify, perf 60fps/37 calls/1626 tris @ wave 4 within baseline); verdict PASS (production/gates.md).
